import { BlockInfoCard } from "@/components/ui/block-info-card"

<BlockInfoCard type="quickbooks" color="#2CA01C" />

Nutzungsanweisungen [#nutzungsanweisungen]

Connect to QuickBooks Online to read and write customers, invoices, and chart-of-accounts entries, or run arbitrary QuickBooks queries. Uses Intuit OAuth 2.0; the company (realmId) is captured from the OAuth callback at sign-in time.

Tools [#tools]

quickbooks_create_bill [#quickbooks_create_bill]

Create a new bill (vendor expense) in QuickBooks Online

Eingabe [#eingabe]

| Parameter  | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                                   |
| ---------- | ------ | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `realmId`  | string | Ja           | QuickBooks company ID (realmId) — captured at OAuth time                       |
| `vendorId` | string | Ja           | Vendor ID for the bill                                                         |
| `lines`    | json   | Ja           | Bill line items (JSON array). Each entry: \{ amount, accountId, description? } |
| `txnDate`  | string | Nein         | Transaction date (YYYY-MM-DD)                                                  |
| `dueDate`  | string | Nein         | Due date (YYYY-MM-DD)                                                          |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe]

| Parameter       | Typ    | Beschreibung                                              |
| --------------- | ------ | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `bill`          | object | Created bill                                              |
| ↳ `Id`          | string | QuickBooks bill ID                                        |
| ↳ `VendorRef`   | object | Vendor reference                                          |
| ↳ `TxnDate`     | string | Transaction date (YYYY-MM-DD)                             |
| ↳ `DueDate`     | string | Due date (YYYY-MM-DD)                                     |
| ↳ `TotalAmt`    | number | Total bill amount                                         |
| ↳ `Balance`     | number | Outstanding balance                                       |
| ↳ `Line`        | array  | Bill line items                                           |
| ↳ `CurrencyRef` | object | Currency reference                                        |
| ↳ `MetaData`    | object | Create/update timestamps                                  |
| ↳ `SyncToken`   | string | Optimistic concurrency token; required for sparse updates |
| `billId`        | string | New bill ID                                               |

quickbooks_create_customer [#quickbooks_create_customer]

Create a new customer in QuickBooks Online

Eingabe [#eingabe-1]

| Parameter      | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                             |
| -------------- | ------ | ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `realmId`      | string | Ja           | QuickBooks company ID (realmId) — captured at OAuth time |
| `displayName`  | string | Ja           | Display name (must be unique within the company)         |
| `companyName`  | string | Nein         | Company name                                             |
| `givenName`    | string | Nein         | First name                                               |
| `familyName`   | string | Nein         | Last name                                                |
| `primaryEmail` | string | Nein         | Primary email address                                    |
| `primaryPhone` | string | Nein         | Primary phone number                                     |
| `notes`        | string | Nein         | Free-form notes                                          |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-1]

| Parameter            | Typ     | Beschreibung                                              |
| -------------------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `customer`           | object  | Created customer                                          |
| ↳ `Id`               | string  | QuickBooks customer ID                                    |
| ↳ `DisplayName`      | string  | Display name shown in lists and forms                     |
| ↳ `CompanyName`      | string  | Company name                                              |
| ↳ `GivenName`        | string  | First name                                                |
| ↳ `FamilyName`       | string  | Last name                                                 |
| ↳ `PrimaryEmailAddr` | object  | Primary email object with `Address`                       |
| ↳ `PrimaryPhone`     | object  | Primary phone object with `FreeFormNumber`                |
| ↳ `Active`           | boolean | Whether the customer is active                            |
| ↳ `Balance`          | number  | Open balance owed by the customer                         |
| ↳ `CurrencyRef`      | object  | Currency reference (`value`, `name`)                      |
| ↳ `Notes`            | string  | Free-form notes                                           |
| ↳ `MetaData`         | object  | Create/update timestamps                                  |
| ↳ `SyncToken`        | string  | Optimistic concurrency token; required for sparse updates |
| `customerId`         | string  | New customer ID                                           |

quickbooks_create_invoice [#quickbooks_create_invoice]

Create a new invoice in QuickBooks Online

Eingabe [#eingabe-2]

| Parameter      | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------- | ------ | ------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `realmId`      | string | Ja           | QuickBooks company ID (realmId) — captured at OAuth time                                                                                                         |
| `customerId`   | string | Ja           | Customer ID to bill                                                                                                                                              |
| `lines`        | json   | Ja           | Invoice line items (JSON array). Each entry: \{ itemId, amount, quantity?, description?, itemName? }. itemId is required (use list\_items to look up valid IDs). |
| `txnDate`      | string | Nein         | Transaction date (YYYY-MM-DD)                                                                                                                                    |
| `dueDate`      | string | Nein         | Due date (YYYY-MM-DD)                                                                                                                                            |
| `customerMemo` | string | Nein         | Memo shown to the customer on the invoice                                                                                                                        |
| `billEmail`    | string | Nein         | Email address to bill (used by Send Invoice flows)                                                                                                               |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-2]

| Parameter        | Typ    | Beschreibung                                              |
| ---------------- | ------ | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `invoice`        | object | Created invoice                                           |
| ↳ `Id`           | string | QuickBooks invoice ID                                     |
| ↳ `DocNumber`    | string | Invoice document number                                   |
| ↳ `TxnDate`      | string | Invoice transaction date (YYYY-MM-DD)                     |
| ↳ `DueDate`      | string | Invoice due date (YYYY-MM-DD)                             |
| ↳ `CustomerRef`  | object | Reference to the customer (`value`, `name`)               |
| ↳ `Line`         | array  | Invoice line items                                        |
| ↳ `TotalAmt`     | number | Total amount of the invoice                               |
| ↳ `Balance`      | number | Outstanding balance on the invoice                        |
| ↳ `CurrencyRef`  | object | Currency reference                                        |
| ↳ `CustomerMemo` | object | Customer-facing memo                                      |
| ↳ `BillEmail`    | object | Billing email object                                      |
| ↳ `EmailStatus`  | string | Email send status (NotSet, NeedToSend, EmailSent)         |
| ↳ `MetaData`     | object | Create/update timestamps                                  |
| ↳ `SyncToken`    | string | Optimistic concurrency token; required for sparse updates |
| `invoiceId`      | string | New invoice ID                                            |

quickbooks_create_item [#quickbooks_create_item]

Create a new item (product or service) in QuickBooks Online

Eingabe [#eingabe-3]

| Parameter         | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                         |
| ----------------- | ------ | ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `realmId`         | string | Ja           | QuickBooks company ID (realmId) — captured at OAuth time             |
| `name`            | string | Ja           | Item name (must be unique within the company)                        |
| `type`            | string | Ja           | Item type — Service or NonInventory (Inventory not supported in MVP) |
| `incomeAccountId` | string | Ja           | Income account ID to associate with the item                         |
| `description`     | string | Nein         | Item description                                                     |
| `unitPrice`       | number | Nein         | Unit price for the item                                              |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-3]

| Parameter             | Typ     | Beschreibung                                              |
| --------------------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `item`                | object  | Created item                                              |
| ↳ `Id`                | string  | QuickBooks item ID                                        |
| ↳ `Name`              | string  | Item name                                                 |
| ↳ `Description`       | string  | Item description                                          |
| ↳ `Type`              | string  | Item type (Service, Inventory, NonInventory)              |
| ↳ `Active`            | boolean | Whether the item is active                                |
| ↳ `UnitPrice`         | number  | Unit price                                                |
| ↳ `IncomeAccountRef`  | object  | Income account reference                                  |
| ↳ `ExpenseAccountRef` | object  | Expense account reference                                 |
| ↳ `AssetAccountRef`   | object  | Asset account reference                                   |
| ↳ `QtyOnHand`         | number  | Quantity on hand (Inventory only)                         |
| ↳ `MetaData`          | object  | Create/update timestamps                                  |
| ↳ `SyncToken`         | string  | Optimistic concurrency token; required for sparse updates |
| `itemId`              | string  | New item ID                                               |

quickbooks_create_payment [#quickbooks_create_payment]

Record a customer payment in QuickBooks Online

Eingabe [#eingabe-4]

| Parameter         | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                             |
| ----------------- | ------ | ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `realmId`         | string | Ja           | QuickBooks company ID (realmId) — captured at OAuth time |
| `customerId`      | string | Ja           | Customer ID making the payment                           |
| `amount`          | number | Ja           | Total payment amount                                     |
| `invoiceId`       | string | Nein         | Optional invoice ID to apply the payment to              |
| `paymentMethodId` | string | Nein         | Payment method ID                                        |
| `txnDate`         | string | Nein         | Transaction date (YYYY-MM-DD)                            |
| `paymentRefNum`   | string | Nein         | Payment reference number (e.g., check number)            |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-4]

| Parameter            | Typ    | Beschreibung                                              |
| -------------------- | ------ | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `payment`            | object | Created payment                                           |
| ↳ `Id`               | string | QuickBooks payment ID                                     |
| ↳ `TxnDate`          | string | Transaction date (YYYY-MM-DD)                             |
| ↳ `CustomerRef`      | object | Customer reference                                        |
| ↳ `TotalAmt`         | number | Total payment amount                                      |
| ↳ `UnappliedAmt`     | number | Unapplied amount                                          |
| ↳ `PaymentMethodRef` | object | Payment method reference                                  |
| ↳ `PaymentRefNum`    | string | Payment reference number                                  |
| ↳ `Line`             | array  | Payment line items / linked transactions                  |
| ↳ `CurrencyRef`      | object | Currency reference                                        |
| ↳ `MetaData`         | object | Create/update timestamps                                  |
| ↳ `SyncToken`        | string | Optimistic concurrency token; required for sparse updates |
| `paymentId`          | string | New payment ID                                            |

quickbooks_list_accounts [#quickbooks_list_accounts]

List chart-of-accounts entries from QuickBooks Online

Eingabe [#eingabe-10]

| Parameter       | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                                 |
| --------------- | ------ | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `realmId`       | string | Ja           | QuickBooks company ID (realmId) — captured at OAuth time     |
| `maxResults`    | number | Nein         | Maximum number of accounts to return (default 100, max 1000) |
| `startPosition` | number | Nein         | Pagination start position (1-indexed)                        |
| `where`         | string | Nein         | Optional WHERE clause (e.g., "AccountType = 'Income'")       |

Ausgabe [#ausgabe-10]

| Parameter          | Typ     | Beschreibung                                              |
| ------------------ | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `accounts`         | array   | Array of accounts                                         |
| ↳ `Id`             | string  | QuickBooks account ID                                     |
| ↳ `Name`           | string  | Account name                                              |
| ↳ `AccountType`    | string  | High-level account type (Income, Expense, etc.)           |
| ↳ `AccountSubType` | string  | Account sub-type                                          |
| ↳ `Classification` | string  | Asset, Liability, Equity, Revenue, or Expense             |
| ↳ `CurrentBalance` | number  | Current account balance                                   |
| ↳ `Active`         | boolean | Whether the account is active                             |
| ↳ `CurrencyRef`    | object  | Currency reference                                        |
| ↳ `SyncToken`      | string  | Optimistic concurrency token; required for sparse updates |
| `totalCount`       | number  | Number of accounts returned                               |

quickbooks_list_bills [#quickbooks_list_bills]

List bills from QuickBooks Online

Eingabe [#eingabe-11]

| Parameter       | Typ    | Erforderlich | Beschreibung                                              |
| --------------- | ------ | ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `realmId`       | string | Ja           | QuickBooks company ID (realmId) — captured at OAuth time  |
| `maxResults`    | number | Nein         | Maximum number of bills to return (default 100, max 1000) |
| `startPosition` | number | Nein         | Pagination start position (1-indexed)                     |
| `where`         | string | Nein         | Optional WHERE clause                                     |
